### Q91. JEE Main 2020 (03 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The magnetic field of a plane electromagnetic wave is  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>B</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo mathvariant="italic">-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi>sin</mi><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mn>200</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>y</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></mfenced><mover><mi>i</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math> . Where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mn>8</mn><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> is the speed of light the corresponding electric filed is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>[</mo><mn>200</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>ct</mi><mo>)</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>[</mo><mn>200</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>ct</mi><mo>)</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>[</mo><mn>200</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>ct</mi><mo>)</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>[</mo><mn>200</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>ct</mi><mo>)</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>[</mo><mn>200</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>ct</mi><mo>)</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><em><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>E</mi><mn mathvariant="italic">0</mn></msub><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mi>c</mi><msub><mi>B</mi><mn mathvariant="italic">0</mn></msub></math></em></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8756;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">E</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mo mathvariant="italic">&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">B</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8214;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">C</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8594;</mo></mover><mo mathvariant="italic">&#8658;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">k</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover><mo mathvariant="italic">&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">i</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="italic">j</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">^</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi>sin</mi><mo>[</mo><mn>200</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>ct</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>[</mo><mn>200</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>ct</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>]</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>-</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math></p>

</details>

### Q92. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

In a plane electromagnetic wave, the directions of electric field and magnetic field are represented by<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>,  respectively. What is the unit vector along direction of propagation of the wave.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>(</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#8214;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q93. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane electromagnetic wave, has frequency of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>10</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Hz</mi></math> and its energy density is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>02</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math> in vacuum. The amplitude of the magnetic field of the wave is close to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>4</mn><msub><mi>&#960;&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>9</mn></msup><mfrac><msup><mi>Nm</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> and speed of light <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>150</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>nT</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>160</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>nT</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>180</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>nT</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>190</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>nT</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>160</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>nT</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Energy Denisty  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfrac><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>&#215;</mo><mtext>&#160;Energy density&#160;</mtext></msqrt></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>02</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>160</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>160</mn><mi>nT</mi></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q94. JEE Main 2020 (09 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

A plane electromagnetic wave is propagating along the direction <math><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> with its polarization along the direction <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>.</mo></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> The correct form of the magnetic field of the wave would be (here <math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math> is an appropriate constant):

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>k</mi><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>ω</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<h2> </h2><p>Polarization of EM Wave means Direction of Its electric Field</p><p>Electric field vector is along <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p>So, unit electric filed vector, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> (Given)</p><p>Direction of wave,<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math>  (Given)</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>  denotes unit magnetic field vector.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>C</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math></p><p>General Form is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>)</mo><mi>cos</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> is Magnitude of Magnetic Field</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>S</mi><mi>o</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac><mi>cos</mi><mfenced><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mfrac><mrow><mover><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q95. JEE Main 2020 (09 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

The electric fields of two plane electromagnetic plane waves in vacuum are given by <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> At <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math> a particle of charge <math><mi>q</mi></math> is at origin with a velocity <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>v</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>08</mn><mi>c</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> ( <math><mi>c</mi></math> is the speed of light in vaccum). The instantaneous force experienced by the particle is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mtext>q</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>0.4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mtext>q</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>0.8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mtext>q</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>0.8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mtext>q</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>0.2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msub><mrow><mtext>E</mtext></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mtext>q</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>0.2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Magnetic field vectors associated with this electromagnetic wave are given by 
<math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>ω</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> &amp; <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi></mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>ω</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>  
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>q</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>q</mi><mi></mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>q</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>  
By putting the value of <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi></mi><mo>&amp;</mo><mi></mi><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>  
The net Lorentz force on the charged particles is 
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>q</mi><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.8</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>ω</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>ω</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>0.2</mn><mi></mi><mi>c</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>ω</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>  
At <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and at <math><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>F</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>q</mi><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>0.2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q96. JEE Main 2020 (08 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane electromagnetic wave of frequency <math><mn>25</mn><mi></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> is propagating in vacuum along the z-direction. At a particular point in space and time, the magnetic filed is given by <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>T</mi><mo>.</mo></math> The corresponding electric field <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is (speed of light <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> )

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1.66</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mrow><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>1.66</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>16</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mrow><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>V</mi></mrow><mi>m</mi></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>15</mn><mover accent="true"><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mrow><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>15</mn><mover accent="true"><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mrow><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>15</mn><mover accent="true"><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mrow><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Given, the electromagnetic wave is propagating in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi></math> i.e., <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> direction.</p><p>The relation between the magnitudes of electric field, magnetic field and the speed of light is given by</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi>E</mi></mfenced><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>C</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi>E</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi>B</mi></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>C</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mi>E</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p><p>We know that the direction of propagation of electromagnetic wave is along <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>.</p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q97. JEE Main 2020 (07 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the magnetic field in a plane electromagnetic wave is given by <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>T</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi></math> then what will be expression for electric field ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>60</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>C</mi><mi></mi></math> (speed of light in vacuum)
<math><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mfrac><mrow><mi>N</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
So,</p><p> <math><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#981;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>3</mn></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>48</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>10</mn></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q98. JEE Main 2019 (12 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

A plane electromagnetic wave having a frequency <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>23.9</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">G</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> propagates along the positive <math><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></math> direction in free space. The peak value of the Electric Field is <math><mn>60</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo></math> Which among the following is the acceptable magnetic field component in the electromagnetic wave?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(B)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>0.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(C)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(D)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>60</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>60</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup></math>  
The magnetic field can be written as, 
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">λ</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>≈</mo><mn>500</mn></math>  
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>≈</mo><mn>1.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup></math>  
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo></mrow></mrow><mn>0.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1.5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>)</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

</details>

### Q99. JEE Main 2019 (12 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

An electromagnetic wave is represented by the electric filed <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo></mrow></mrow></math> Taking unit vectors in <math><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi></math> and <math><mi>z</mi></math> directions to be <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi></math> the directions of propogations <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>,</mo></math> is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mi>i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The standard equation of electromagnetic wave <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mover><mi>n</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>sin</mi><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>k</mi><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math></p><p>here <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo></math>represents the direction of propagation wave.</p>On comparing the given equation with the standard equation, the direction of propagation is along line <math><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>z</mi></math> . Thus, unit vector along the direction of propagation is,
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

</details>

### Q100. JEE Main 2019 (10 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The electric field of a plane electromagnetic wave is given by
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
The corresponding magnetic field <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is then given by:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>)</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">ω</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>)</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mover accent="true"><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mi>j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mtext>&#160;</mtext><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q101. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

Sunlight of intensity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>50</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></math>is incident normally on the surface of a solar panel. Some part of incident energy (<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>25</mn><mo>%</mo></math>) is reflected from the surface and the rest is absorbed. The force exerted on <math><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math> surface area will be close to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>15</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>20</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>10</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>35</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>20</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The pressure exerted by the photons</p><p> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.75</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mi>I</mi><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>0.25</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>I</mi></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac></math> ( <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> = pressure, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> = speed of light, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi></math> = intensity )

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1.25</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="italic">I</mi><mi mathvariant="italic">c</mi></mfrac></math>

 We know the force can be computed using

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">=</mo><mi>P</mi><mo mathvariant="italic">&#215;</mo><mi>A</mi></math> </p><p>( <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi></math> = force, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>P</mi></math> = pressure, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>A</mi></math> = area )

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1.25</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mrow><mn>50</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mn>8</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>F</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>20.83</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math></p>

</details>

### Q102. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

The magnetic field of a plane electromagnetic wave is given by <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, where <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>. The <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>RMS</mi></math> value of the force experienced by a stationary charge <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> at <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is closest to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0.1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>0.9</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>–</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0.6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>0.6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>
Electric filed associated with it is,
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
Here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> is the speed of light in vacuum.
At <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">F</mi></mrow><mrow><mtext>rms</mtext></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
Here <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> is speed of light in vacuum.
<math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>0.81</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0.0036</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>0.6</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>

</details>

### Q103. JEE Main 2019 (08 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The magnetic field of an electromagnetic wave is given by:
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>1.6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi></math>
The associated electric field will be:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4.8</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4.8</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4.8</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4.8</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4.8</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given magnetic field, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>1.6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math>
The relation between magnetic field and electric field is <math><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mo>)</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>1.6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>4.8</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><msqrt><mn>5</mn></msqrt></math>
The direction of light propagation will be along <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> .
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>4.8</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q104. JEE Main 2019 (08 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane electromagnetic wave travels in free space along the x-direction. The electric field component of the wave at a particular point of space and time is <math><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math> along y-direction. Its corresponding magnetic field component, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> would be:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> along z-direction
- **(B)** <math><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>  along y-direction
- **(C)** <math><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>  along z-direction
- **(D)** <math><mi> </mi><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>  along x-direction

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math> along z-direction**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>  
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is along <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> -direction 
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>×</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>  
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>  
<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover></math> is along <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>  
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math> along <math><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math> -direction

</details>
