### Q105. JEE Main 2019 (12 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The mean intensity of radiation on the surface of the Sun is about <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi>W</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo></math> The rms value of the corresponding magnetic field is closest to:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi>T</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi>T</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msubsup><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn><mrow><mi>&#160;</mi><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>C</mi></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mrow><mi mathvariant="bold">r</mi><mi mathvariant="bold">m</mi><mi mathvariant="bold">s</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><msub><mi>&#956;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>I</mi></mrow><mi>C</mi></mfrac></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8776;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>T</mi></math>

</details>

### Q106. JEE Main 2019 (11 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A $27 \mathrm{~mW}$ laser beam has a cross-sectional area of $10 \mathrm{~mm}^{2}$. The magnitude of the maximum electric field in this electromagnetic wave is given by:

[Given permittivity of space $\epsilon_{0}=9 \times 10^{-12} \mathrm{SI}$ units, Speed of light $\left.\mathrm{c}=3 \times 10^{8} \mathrm{~m} / \mathrm{s}\right]$

**Options:**

- **(A)** $2 \mathrm{kV} / \mathrm{m}$
- **(B)** $0.7 \mathrm{kV} / \mathrm{m}$
- **(C)** $1 \mathrm{kV} / \mathrm{m}$
- **(D)** $1.4 \mathrm{kV} / \mathrm{m}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $1.4 \mathrm{kV} / \mathrm{m}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

EM wave intensity

$\Rightarrow \mathrm{I}=\frac{\text { Power }}{\text { Area }}=\frac{1}{2} \varepsilon_{0} \mathrm{E}_{0}^{2} \mathrm{c}$

[where $\mathrm{E}_{0}=$ maximum electric field] $]$

$\Rightarrow \frac{27 \times 10^{-3}}{10 \times 10^{-6}}=\frac{1}{2} \times 9 \times 10^{-12} \times \mathrm{E}_{0}^{2} \times 3 \times 10^{8}$

$\Rightarrow \mathrm{E}_{0}=\sqrt{2} \times 10^{3} \mathrm{kV} / \mathrm{m}=1.4 \mathrm{kV} / \mathrm{m}$

</details>

### Q107. JEE Main 2019 (11 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

An electromagnetic wave of intensity $50 \mathrm{Wm}^{-2}$ enters in a medium of refractive index 'n' without any loss. The ratio of the magnitudes of electric fields, and the ratio of the magnitudes of magnetic fields of the wave before and after entering into the medium are respectively, given by :

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathrm{n}}}, \frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathrm{n}}}\right)$
- **(B)** $(\sqrt{n}, \sqrt{n})$
- **(C)** $\left(\sqrt{n}, \frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)$
- **(D)** $\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}, \sqrt{n}\right)$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $\left(\sqrt{n}, \frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)$**

**Detailed Solution:**

The speed of electromagnetic wave in free space is given by

$\mathrm{C}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mu_{0} \epsilon_{0}}}$

In medium, $\mathbf{v}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathrm{k} \in_{0} \mu_{0}}} \ldots$ (ii)

Dividing equation (i) by(ii), we get $\therefore \frac{\mathrm{C}}{\mathrm{V}}=\sqrt{\mathrm{k}}=\mathrm{n}$

$\frac{1}{2} \epsilon_{0} \mathrm{E}_{0}^{2} \mathrm{C}=$ intensity $=\frac{1}{2} \epsilon_{0} \mathrm{kE}^{2} \mathrm{v}$

$\therefore \mathrm{E}_{0}^{2} \mathrm{C}=\mathrm{kE}^{2} \mathrm{v}$

$\Rightarrow \frac{E_{0}^{2}}{E^{2}}=\frac{k V}{C}=\frac{n^{2}}{n} \Rightarrow \frac{E_{0}}{E}=\sqrt{n}$

similarly

$\frac{\mathrm{B}_{0}^{2} \mathrm{C}}{2 \mu_{0}}=\frac{\mathrm{B}^{2} \mathrm{v}}{2 \mu_{0}} \Rightarrow \frac{\mathrm{B}_{0}}{\mathrm{~B}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\mathrm{n}}}$

</details>

### Q108. JEE Main 2019 (10 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

The electric field of a plane polarized electromagnetic wave in free space at time <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is given by the expression <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>. The magnetic field <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>z</mi><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is given by (<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> is the velocity of light.)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi>cos</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi>cos</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi>cos</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>10</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>cos</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mover><mrow><mi>K</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover><mrow><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>r</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> At any time <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>t</mi></math>,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>K</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mi>k</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>10</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>5</mn></mfrac></math>

Also, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mover><mi>c</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi>f</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi>c</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#955;</mi></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd><mtd><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>0</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>x</mi></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>y</mi></msub></mtd><mtd><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>z</mi></msub></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mn>10</mn></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#969;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>5</mn></mfrac></mfenced></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>c</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>z</mi></msub><mo>-</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>x</mi></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0.6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>0.8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

Comparing the coefficients on both sides,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>z</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>0.6</mn><mo>;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mi>B</mi><mi>x</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>0.8</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mover><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>10</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>0.8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>0.6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac></math>

So the magnetic field,<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>tesla</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q109. JEE Main 2019 (10 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If the magnetic field of a plane electromagnetic wave is given by (The speed of light <math><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#160;</mi><mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow></mrow></math>)

<math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>15</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math> then the maximum electric field associated with it is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>4</mn></msup><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>4</mn></msup><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4.5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>4</mn></msup><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>4</mn></msup><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>4</mn></msup><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>We know that relation between electric field E and magnetic field B is given by:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>E</mi><mi>B</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mi>c</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>4</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p>

</details>

---

### Q110. JEE Main 2019 (09 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The energy associated with electric field is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> and with magnetic field is <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math> for an electromagnetic wave in free space. Then:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&gt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&lt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>U</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The average energy density of electric field is given by</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>u</mi><mi>E</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>&#949;</mi><msup><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mn>2</mn></msup></math></p><p>Here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> is the maximum value of electric field.</p><p>The average energy density of magnetic field is given by</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>u</mi><mi>B</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>Here, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math> is the maximum value of magnetic field.</p><p>The speed of light is given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mi>&#956;</mi><mi>&#949;</mi></mrow></mfrac></math></p><p>Also, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></math></p><p>So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>u</mi><mi>E</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>c</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow></mfrac><msup><mfenced><mrow><mi>c</mi><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#956;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>u</mi><mi>B</mi></msub></math></p><p>As the energy density of electric field and magnetic field are equal, the energy associated for equal volume will also be equal.</p>

</details>

---

### Q111. JEE Main 2019 (09 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

A plane electromagnetic wave of frequency <math><mn>50&#160;</mn><mi></mi><mi>M</mi><mi>H</mi><mi>z</mi></math> travels in free space along the positive <math><mi>x</mi></math> - direction. At a particular point in space and time, <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>6.3</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mrow><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi>m</mi><mo>,</mo></mrow></mrow></math> The corresponding magnetic field <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi><mo>,</mo></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> at that point will be:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2.1</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>18.9</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>18.9</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mi>T</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>6.3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mi>T</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>2.1</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mi>T</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

As we know <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>E</mi><mi>B</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>C</mi></math>

<math><mi>E</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>6.3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi>V</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi>m</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>E</mi><mi>C</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>6.3</mn><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi>T</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2.1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi>T</mi></math>

</details>

---

### Q112. JEE Main 2018 (16 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

A plane electromagnetic wave of wavelength <math><mi>&#955;</mi></math> has an intensity <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi></math>. It is propagating along the positive <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Y</mi><mo>-</mo></math>direction. The allowed expressions for the electric and magnetic fields are given by:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi></msqrt><mi>cos</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mi>E</mi><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi></msqrt><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mi>E</mi><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi></msqrt><mi>cos</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mi>E</mi><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mi>I</mi><mrow><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi></msqrt><mi>cos</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, 

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mi>E</mi><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi></msqrt><mi>cos</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mi>&#955;</mi></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mi>E</mi><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

If <math><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></math> is the amplitude of the electric field then the intensity of the electromagnetic wave is given as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msubsup><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo>&#215;</mo><mi>c</mi></math>. From the above relation, we get <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>I</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>. The relation between electric and magnetic fields is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi></mfrac></math>. The direction of <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>E</mi> <mo stretchy="true">&#8594;</mo> </mover> <mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"> <mi>B</mi> <mo stretchy="true">&#8594;</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math> will be along <math><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>.

</details>

### Q113. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr Shift 2 Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

A plane polarized monochromatic EM wave is travelling a vacuum along $z$ direction such that at $\mathrm{t}=\mathrm{t}_1$ it is found that the electric field is zero at a spatial point $z_1$. The next zero that occurs in its neighbourhood is at $z_2$. The frequency of the electromagnetic wave is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\frac{3 \times 10^8}{\left|z_2-z_1\right|}$
- **(B)** $\frac{6 \times 10^8}{\left|\mathrm{z}_2-\mathrm{z}_1\right|}$
- **(C)** $\frac{1.5 \times 10^8}{\left|\mathrm{z}_2-\mathrm{z}_1\right|}$
- **(D)** $\frac{1}{t_1+\frac{\left|z_2-z_1\right|}{3 \times 10_8}}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $\frac{3 \times 10^8}{\left|z_2-z_1\right|}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Using $E=E_0-e^i(k z-\omega t)$
Given, at $t=t_1, z=z_1, E=0$
the next zero that occurs in it's neighborhood is at $z_2$, the frequency of the electromagnetic wave at $t_2$
$$
\begin{aligned}
&e^{i\left(k z_1-\omega t_1\right)}=e^{i\left(k z_2-\omega t_2\right)} \\
&k z_1-\omega t_1=k z_2-\omega t_2 \\
&\left(t_2-t_1\right) \omega=k\left(z-z_1\right) \\
&\text { where } k=\frac{2 \pi}{\lambda}=2 \pi v \\
&\left(t_2-t_1\right)=\frac{2 \pi}{\lambda \times 2 \pi v}\left(z_2-z_1\right)
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&\quad=\frac{1}{x \times v}\left(z_2-z_1\right) \\
&\Rightarrow \lambda \times v=\frac{\left(z_2-z_1\right)}{\left(t_2-t_1\right)}=C \\
&\quad\left(t_2-t_1\right)=\frac{\left(z_2-z_1\right)}{C} \\
&\text { Frequency is } f \propto \frac{1}{t} \text { then } \frac{1}{\left(t_2-t_1\right)}=\frac{C}{\left(z_2-z_1\right)} \\
&\therefore \text { Frequency, } f=\frac{3 \times 10^8}{\left(z_2-z_1\right)}
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>

### Q114. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr)

It is polarized along the <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> direction. The acceptance form the magnetic field is:

**Options:**

- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mi> </mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">Cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>.</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> should have given direction of wave propagation,<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>K</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#8741;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi></math>
<math><mo></mo><mn></mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>K</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#8741;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi></math>. 

Wave propagation vector <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>K</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> should be along <math><mfrac><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>j</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>.

</details>

### Q116. JEE Main 2018 (08 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

An EM wave from air enters a medium. The electric fields are <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>01</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>v</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi>&#160;</mi></math> in air and <math><msub><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>02</mn></mrow></msub><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>c</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mi></mi></math>in medium, where the wave number k and frequency <math><mi>v</mi></math> refer to their values in the air. The medium is non-magnetic. If <math><msub><mrow><mi>&#1013;</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></math> and <math><msub><mrow><mi>&#1013;</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></math> refer to relative permittivities of air and medium respectively, which of the following options, is correct?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi> </mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mo>∨</mo></mrow><mrow><mi>a</mi><mi>i</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mo>∨</mo></mrow><mrow><mi>m</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>d</mi></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>c</mi><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></msqrt></mrow><mrow><msqrt><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi> </mi></math></br></br><math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>ϵ</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q117. JEE Main 2017 (09 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

The electric field component of a monochromatic radiation is given by <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>cos</mi><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>, Its magnetic field <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is then given by:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mi>sin</mi><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi><mi>sin</mi><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mi>cos</mi><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mi>sin</mi><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi><mi>cos</mi><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>-2</mn><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mi>sin</mi><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi><mi>sin</mi><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mi>sin</mi><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi><mi>sin</mi><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>K</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mi>d</mi><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mi>K</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>K</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>&#8747;</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mfrac><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>C</mi></math>

<math><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow></math></p><p>Propagation of EM radiation, Electric field oscillation and Magnetic field oscillation are perpendicular to each other.</p><p>Since propagation of EM radiation is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo></math>axis and electric field oscillation is along <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>z</mi><mo>-</mo></math>axis, the Magnetic field oscillation is along <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>y</mi><mo>-</mo></math>axis.</p>

</details>

---

### Q118. JEE Main 2017 (08 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Magnetic field in a plane electromagnetic wave is given by,

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>. Expression for corresponding electric field will be:

(Where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> is speed of light)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi></mfrac><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>For electromagnetic wave speed of light is given by

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mfrac></math>
Electric field is expressed as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>i</mi><mi>s</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#8869;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mtext>to&#160;direction</mtext><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mtext>and&#160;direction</mtext><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>f</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>

The expression of electric field is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mi>c</mi><msub><mi>B</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mo>(</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>w</mi><mi>t</mi><mo>)</mo><mover><mi>k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q119. JEE Main 2016 (10 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Consider an electromagnetic wave propagating in vacuum. Choose the correct statement:

**Options:**

- **(A)** For an electromagnetic wave propagating in <math><mo>+</mo><mi>y</mi></math> direction the electric field is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and the magnetic field is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(B)** For an electromagnetic wave propagating in + y direction the electric field is  <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> and the magnetic field is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>→</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>yz</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math>
- **(C)** For an electromagnetic wave propagating in + x direction the electric field is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and the magnetic field is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>y</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>z</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** For an electromagnetic wave propagating in + x direction the electric field is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and the magnetic field is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) For an electromagnetic wave propagating in + x direction the electric field is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and the magnetic field is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>y</mi><mi>z</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>y</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>z</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Wave in x - direction means <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> should be function of x and t

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>y</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mover accent="true"><mi>z</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8869;</mo><mfenced><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi>y</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mover accent="true"><mi>z</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>|</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mover><mi>x</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>&#160;</mi></math></p></blockquote>

</details>

---

### Q120. JEE Main 2015 (11 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For plane electromagnetic waves propagating in the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>+</mo><mi>z</mi></math>-direction, which one of the following combinations gives the correct possible direction for <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> and <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> field respectively?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The direction of propagation of the electromagnetic waves is along <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math>.

Also <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

When <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is along <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

And <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> is along <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

Then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#215;</mo><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#215;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>13</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> is along +ve direction also <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover><mi>E</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>&#183;</mo><mover><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>

</details>

---

### Q121. JEE Main 2015 (10 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

An electromagnetic wave travelling in the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo></math> direction has frequency of  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>14</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math>  and electric field amplitude of  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>27</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>&#8211;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>  oscillates in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Y</mi><mo>-</mo></math>direction. From the options given below, which one describes the magnetic field for this wave?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi><mo>)</mo><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>sin</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>14</mn></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mover accent="true"><mi>B</mi><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi>T</mi><mo>)</mo><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>sin</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>14</mn></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>&#160;</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>B</mi> <mo stretchy="true">&#8594;</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">j</mi><mo>^</mo></mover><mi>sin</mi><mi>2&#960;</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mrow><mn>1.5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>14</mn></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>B</mi> <mo stretchy="true">&#8594;</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mtext>^</mtext></mover><mi>sin2&#960;</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mrow><mn>1.5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>14</mn></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>B</mi> <mo stretchy="true">&#8594;</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mtext>^</mtext></mover><mi>sin2&#960;</mi><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mrow><mn>1.5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>14</mn></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

When undefined

Then <math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>B</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="italic">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo></mrow></math><math>&#160;<mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mi>k</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8722;</mo><mi>&#969;</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow></mrow> </math>

Of light in travelling along <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></math> then <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover></math> in either along <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>j</mi> <mo stretchy="true">^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math> or <math> <mrow> <mover accent="true"> <mi>k</mi> <mo stretchy="true">^</mo> </mover> </mrow> </math>.

<math><mo>&#8756;</mo></math> speed of light <math><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>C</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfrac><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>E</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>27</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>T</mi></math>

also, <math><mi>&#969;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">f</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>&#960;</mi><mi>&#160;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math> <mi>k</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mi>&#969;</mi> <mi>c</mi> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mn>14</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mn>8</mn> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </math>

<math> <mo>=</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow> <mrow> <mn>1.5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> <mo>)</mo></mrow> </math>

Looking into the option the correct

Answer is <math><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow><mo>&#8594;</mo></mover><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1.5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>6</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>14</mn></mrow></msup><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mover accent="true"><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mo>^</mo></mover></mrow></mrow></math>

</details>

---

### Q122. JEE Main 2015 (04 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `2`

<p>A red <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>L</mi><mi>E</mi><mi>D</mi></math> emits light at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>watt</mi></math> uniformly around it. The amplitude of the electric field of the light at a distance of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math> from the diode is:</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>75</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>73</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>45</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>48</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>45</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/P_35_Ans_Correction.jpg" style="width: 122px; height: 126px;" />

For a point source of power = P, then intensity at a point at a separation x from the source is, <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>o</mi><mi>w</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>r</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>A</mi><mi>r</mi><mi>e</mi><mi>a</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></math><math><mo>&#8757;</mo></math> The average intensity of E.M. wave is given by, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>c</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><msubsup><mi>E</mi><mi>o</mi><mn>2</mn></msubsup></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>P</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mi>c</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi>&#960;</mi><msub><mi>&#949;</mi><mn>0</mn></msub></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>9</mn></msup><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>P</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0.1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">W</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#160;</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi></math>, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi>E</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0.1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>9</mn></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac></msqrt><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>6</mn></msqrt><mo>=</mo><mn>2.45</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">V</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

---

### Q123. JEE Main 2014 (12 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `3`

A lamp emits monochromatic green light uniformly in all directions. The lamp is $3 \%$ efficient in converting electrical power to electromagnetic waves and consumes $100 \mathrm{~W}$ of power. The amplitude of the electric field associated with the electromagnetic radiation at a distance of $5 \mathrm{~m}$ from the lamp will be nearly:

**Options:**

- **(A)** $1.34 \mathrm{~V} / \mathrm{m}$
- **(B)** $2.68 \mathrm{~V} / \mathrm{m}$
- **(C)** $4.02 \mathrm{~V} / \mathrm{m}$
- **(D)** $5.36 \mathrm{~V} / \mathrm{m}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) $2.68 \mathrm{~V} / \mathrm{m}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Wavelength of monochromatic green light
$$
\begin{aligned}
&=5.5 \times 10^{-5} \mathrm{~cm} \\
&\text { Intensity I }=\frac{\text { Power }}{\text { Area }} \\
&=\frac{100 \times(3 / 100)}{4 \pi(5)^2} \\
&=\frac{3}{100 \pi} \mathrm{Wm}^{-2}
\end{aligned}
$$
Now, half of this intensity (I) belongs to electric field and half of that to magnetic field, therefore,
$$
\begin{aligned}
&\frac{\mathrm{I}}{2}=\frac{1}{4} \varepsilon_0 \mathrm{E}_0^2 \mathrm{C} \\
&\text { or } \mathrm{E}_0=\sqrt{\frac{2 \mathrm{I}}{\varepsilon_0 \mathrm{C}}}
\end{aligned}
$$

$$
\begin{aligned}
&=\sqrt{\frac{2 \times\left(\frac{3}{100} \pi\right)}{\left(\frac{1}{4 \pi \times 9 \times 10^9}\right) \times\left(3 \times 10^8\right.}} \\
&=\sqrt{\frac{6}{25} \times 30}=\sqrt{7.2} \\
&\therefore \mathrm{E}_0=2.68 \mathrm{~V} / \mathrm{m}
\end{aligned}
$$

</details>
